Step 7 - Thought:                                                                                                                                                                                       
I will now retrieve the fee record with ID 384 from fees_data. This record will give us the original relative fee (the "rate") and fixed_amount. Then we can simulate a change where the relative fee is
changed to 1, compute for each transaction (for the merchant in January 2023) the difference in fee charged ("delta") using the new and the original fee structure, and then sum up these differences.  
However, we must decide on a fee formula.                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
Based on common practices and the structure of fees.json, a plausible fee formula is:                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
  fee = fixed_amount + (relative_fee_rate/10000) * eur_amount                                                                                                                                           
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
Here relative_fee_rate is given in basis points (e.g., a rate of 19 means 0.19% fee) because fixed_amount is a small decimal and amounts in euros are likely in the range of tens or hundreds.          
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
Hence:                                                                                                                                                                                                  
  old_fee = fixed_amount + (original_rate / 10000) * eur_amount                                                                                                                                         
  new_fee = fixed_amount + (1 / 10000) * eur_amount                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
So the delta for each transaction is:                                                                                                                                                                   
  delta = new_fee - old_fee = ((1 - original_rate) / 10000) * eur_amount                                                                                                                                
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
We then sum this delta over all transactions in January 2023 for Belles_cookbook_store.                                                                                                                 
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
I will proceed with these assumptions and calculations.

Step 7 - Thought:                                                                                                                                                                                       
We now have fee384 record: fixed_amount = 0.05 and original rate = 14 (interpreted in basis points). We assume the fee formula is:                                                                      
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
  fee = fixed_amount + (rate/10000) × eur_amount                                                                                                                                                        
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
For each transaction, the original fee charged is:                                                                                                                                                      
  old_fee = 0.05 + (14/10000) × eur_amount                                                                                                                                                              
and with the modified fee, it becomes:                                                                                                                                                                  
  new_fee = 0.05 + (1/10000) × eur_amount                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
Thus, the delta (the difference) per transaction is:                                                                                                                                                    
  delta = new_fee − old_fee = ((1 − 14)/10000) × eur_amount = (−13/10000) × eur_amount                                                                                                                  
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
We now filter the payments for Belles_cookbook_store in January 2023. Since payments.csv provides a column day_of_year, we treat days 1–31 as January. Then we sum up the delta over all these          
transactions.                                                                                                                                                                                           
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
Code:                                                                                                                                                                                                   
```py                                                                                                                                                                                                   
import pandas as pd                                                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
# Filter transactions for Belles_cookbook_store in January 2023 (day_of_year in 1..31)                                                                                                                  
belles_payments = payments_df[(payments_df['merchant'] == 'Belles_cookbook_store') &                                                                                                                    
                              (payments_df['year'] == 2023) &                                                                                                                                           
                              (payments_df['day_of_year'] <= 31)]                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
# Compute the delta for each transaction.                                                                                                                                                               
# Delta = (-13/10000) * eur_amount                                                                                                                                                                      
belles_payments['delta'] = (-13/10000) * belles_payments['eur_amount']                                                                                                                                  
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
# Sum the delta                                                                                                                                                                                         
total_delta = belles_payments['delta'].sum()                                                                                                                                                            
print("Total Delta:", total_delta)                                                                                                                                                                      
```<end_code>

- **Description**: Synthetic dataset of payment transactions processed by the Payments Processor.
- **Columns**:
  - `psp_reference`: Unique payment identifier (ID).

  - `day_of_year`: Day of the year of payment initiation (Numeric).
  - `is_credit`: Credit or Debit card indicator (Categorical).

# Load payments.csv fully (or at least enough rows)                                                                                                                                                     
payments = pd.read_csv("data/context/payments.csv")                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                        
# Filter for Belles_cookbook_store, year 2023, and day_of_year 10.                                                                                                                                      
filtered_payments = payments[(payments["merchant"]=="Belles_cookbook_store") &                                                                                                                          
                             (payments["year"]==2023) &                                                                                                                                                 
                             (payments["day_of_year"]==10)]                                                                                                                                             
print("Filtered Payments for Belles_cookbook_store on day 10, 2023:")                                                                                                                                   
print(filtered_payments)                                                                                                                                                                                
```<end_code>
